Bid Number: GEM/2026/B/7416997 Dated 07.04.2026
Annexure 01 – Project Details (Scope of Work)
1. Broad Scope of work:
BarodaSun Technologies Limited (“BSTL” or “the Company”), a public sector technology
services company, proposes to select a Service Provider for Supply, Implementation &
Maintenance of Human Resource Management system.
BSTL for this purpose, invites proposal from prospective bidders. Bidder who is interested
in participating in this RFP must fulfil the eligibility criteria mentioned in the RFP and also
in a position to comply with the Scope of Work in the document to provide the required
solution. Apart from above the bidder must also agree to all our terms & conditions
mentioned under this RFP.
The proposed Human Resource Management System (HRMS) solution should be aligned
with, but not limited to, the following key features and technical and functional capabilities:
1. Employee Database Management
1.1 Digital Employee Records & Document Repository: The system should store
complete digital records of every employee, including appointment letters, ID proofs,
certificates, and HR documents.
1.2 Core HRMS (Organization, People, Job data, Position, Job, Contact, Dependents) &
Employee Enrolment: The system should manage all Job Data actions, including
person profiles, personal information, biodata, positions etc. It should have
functionality to enroll and maintain complete official records such as name, employee
ID, date of joining, photo, and update history. The system should have functionality
to create category i.e. Department, Designation, Division, Projects, Grade, Locality
etc and also generate employee, EC No., biodata and generic employee lists.
1.3 Personal Details Management: The system should capture address, contact details,
emergency contacts, family information, qualifications, skills, and bank details with
change history.
1.4 Alerts and Notifications: Systematic alerts and notifications in form of emails to
employees and HR/administrative department to update documents, profile
completion, pending updates, etc.
1.5 Multiple Employee Categories: The system should support input and storage of data
for different categories of employees viz Regular, Contractual, and Deputed staff with
relevant information fields.
1.6 Organization Development: The system should enable creation and management of
departments, locations, positions, and job codes. It should maintain the
organizational structure, hierarchy mapping , workflow mapping and reporting
relationships.
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of Human
Resource Management system
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.
Page 1 of 11

Bid Number: GEM/2026/B/7416997 Dated 07.04.2026
2. Attendance & Leave Management
2.1 Biometric/Digital Attendance Integration: The system should integrate with biometric
or digital attendance systems to mark attendance of the employee, which will have
functionality to capture thumb impression, IRIS etc. as approved by Government of
India, Government of Maharashtra.
2.2 Office Hours & Holiday Calendar: Maintain office timings, weekly offs, and annual
holiday list.
2.3 Attendance & Leave Reports: There should be functionality to generate daily,
monthly, and yearly attendance summaries and leave details.
2.4 Leave Rules Configuration & Approvals: Creates master BSTL leave rules, allows
online leave application. Approval Level Creation to manage leave process.
Paperless functionality for leave Application by the employees and approval by their
managers with effect to automatic leave ledger update.
2.5 Real-Time Attendance Dashboard & Timesheet: Shows live attendance status, late
marks, early exits. There should be provision for timesheet entries for employees as
per projects. Allow attendance punching with In /Out daily, monthly, yearly reports.
2.6 Attendance Management: Defines rules for work-from-home, half-day, late marking,
deductions etc as per BSTL’s attendance / leave rules.
3. Payroll, Compensation and Reimbursement & Claims
3.1 CTC Structure & Salary Master: The system should manage employee salary
components and structure.
3.2 Payroll Processing & Statutory Deductions: The system should have provision for
automated payroll processing, tax deductions (TDS, PF, ESI etc). The system should
be integrated with accounting/finance systems, leave details and attendance master
for automatic salary calculations and arrears calculations.
3.3 Investment Declarations & Tax Computation: Allows uploading tax-proof documents
and calculating monthly TDS.
3.4 Salary Slips & Disbursement: Generates payslips and maintains historical salary
records.
3.5 Arrears & Adjustment Reports: Supports arrear calculations and related reporting.
3.6 Claims & Reimbursements – The HRMS should allow employees to submit claims
including TA / DA claims and reimbursement requests as per their eligibility and policy
guidelines. The system should support upload of required documents, route requests
through an approval workflow, and automatically integrate approved claims with the
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of Human
Resource Management system
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.
Page 2 of 11

Bid Number: GEM/2026/B/7416997 Dated 07.04.2026
payroll or payment system for timely settlement. It should have provision to mark
members on leave and reroute claims selectively. It should also maintain a complete
record of all claims with an audit trail.

4. Employee Self Service & Manager Self Service
4.1 Web and mobile application for employees and managers.
4.2 Employee Self-Service: Employees can apply for leave, view payslips, update data,
etc.
4.3 Manager Self-Service: Managers can approve leave, monitor attendance, and
access reports.
4.4 Notifications: Sends alerts for approvals and pending tasks.
5. Performance Management
5.1 Goal Setting & KPI Tracking: The system should enable creation and tracking of
KPIs and goals.
5.2 Appraisal Cycles & Feedback: The system should support Appraisals cycles,
feedback mechanism and process performance-linked pay.
6. Recruitment & Onboarding
6.1 Job Requisition & Posting: Allows managers to raise hiring requests and HR to post
jobs.
6.2 Applicant Tracking System (ATS) & Candidate Tracking: Manages online
applications, candidate tracking system, shortlisting, and selection stages.
6.3 Interview Scheduling & Selection: Supports scheduling, feedback, and offer letter
generation.
6.4 Onboarding Workflow: Handles document uploads, verification, and employee code
generation.
7. Separation & Exit Management
7.1 Resignation Submission: Employees can submit resignations online or on other
separation reasons viz. Demise, Dismissal, etc, the system should follow the exit
process fully automated through workflow including requisite clearances.
7.2 Exit Forms & Clearances: System manages digital exit forms and no-dues
processes.
7.3 Notice Period & Shortfall Calculation: The system should automatically calculate the
notice period, shortfall days with systematic notification / alerts.
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of Human
Resource Management system
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.
Page 3 of 11

Bid Number: GEM/2026/B/7416997 Dated 07.04.2026
7.4 Full & Final Settlement: The system should automatically compute all dues and
recoveries and calculation of full and final settlement.
7.5 Exit Interviews: Records exit feedback.
8. Analytics & Reports
8.1 HR Dashboards: Displays headcount, attendance, attrition, leave usage, etc.
8.2 The system should have ability to generate reports / MIS from system as and when
required. The system should provide flexibility in choosing different headers for
generating various reports.
9. Intimation / Permission:
9.1 Employee Intimation and Permission module. The system should have provision
for employee to request for different types of Intimation / Permission.
10. Confirmation Process:
10.1 The system should generate the list of employees to be confirmed, automatic
workflow with escalation features. On confirmation the record should be updated
automatically in job data.
11. Notice Board / Circulars / Intimation
11.1 The system should have provision for Notice board / Circulars / Intimation tool –
where Admin can post notices/ circulars / letters / communication etc to the target
audience.
12. Full and Final Settlement
12.1 Full and Final Settlement of employees separating from BSTL and calculation of
tax. The FnF should also display any pending dues of the employee. Payment of
F&F records and reconciliation.

13. Technical Requirements
a. Web-based and mobile-enabled architecture
b. Cloud-based deployment.
c. High availability, scalability, and disaster recovery support.
d. Role-based access control and data security as per prevailing standards.
e. Compatibility with modern browsers and operating systems.
14. Training (Offline / Online)
a. User training for HR, IT, and administrative staff
b. Preparation of user manuals and training materials
c. Post-implementation support and capacity building
15. Support and Maintenance
a. Comprehensive warranty and AMC support post go-live
b. Helpdesk support (email, ticketing, remote assistance)
c. Periodic upgrades, patches, and system performance reviews

3. Implementation & Customization
Bidder may require doing some customization in existing module of their software to meet
the requirement of BSLT which should be cover as part of implementation. The system

should provide Confidentiality, Integrity, Authentication and Non-Repudiation as per
current standard (Signing, Hashing, Time-stamping and Encryption).
Bidder as part of the implementation phase shall have to provide a comprehensive handson online / Offline training to BSTL officials at the location prescribed by the BSTL. The
objective of training would be to make an individual familiarise with solution & to get start
working on it. The training shall cover the complete process. The successful completion of
the training would be deemed as executed as part of acceptance of Project Sign off.
After successful completion of the implementation phase, Bidder is liable to provide Offline
/ onsite training to BSTL when required during the contract period. There will not be any
cost borne by BSTL for any training.
All out of pocket expenses, traveling, boarding and lodging expenses for the entire life of
the contract should be a part of the commercial bid submitted by the vendor to the BSTL.
No extra costs on account of any items or services or by way of any out of pocket
expenses, including travel, boarding and lodging etc. will be payable by BSTL. The vendor
cannot take the plea of omitting any charges or costs and later lodge a claim on the BSTL
for the same.
The vendor have to arrange for customization if required in the system to meet the changes
suggested by CVC/ RBI/ Govt. of India/ regulatory authorities/BSTL towards compliance
without any additional cost during the contract period
However, if any changes suggested by BSTL after implementation and one time
customization of the system, BSTL will freeze the manpower effort, after mutual discussion
with the successful vendor.
Vendor need to freeze and sign SOW (scope of work) for customization required by BSTL
on mutual discussion as a payment milestone.
4. Data Segregation and Security Audits:
BSTL's data should be completely segregated within the Cloud environment. The vendor
has to submit CERT-IN empanelled auditor confirmation on BSTL’s data segregation
controls complying to the regulatory norms.
5. Vulnerability Assessment and Penetration Testing (VAPT):
The Vendor shall conduct quarterly vulnerability assessments (VA) on the Cloud
application and its underlying infrastructure.
In addition to the above vulnerability assessments, the Vendor shall conduct Yearly
comprehensive Penetration Testing (PT) on the Cloud application and its underlying
infrastructure.
The Vendor shall provide to the BSTL with a detailed compliance report outlining the
findings of the vulnerability assessments and Penetration Testing, including identified
vulnerabilities, the associated risk level, and the remediation.
However, Bidder must support if BSTL wants to conduct the above assessment through
its technical vendor as and when need arises.
6. Data Migration:

7. Data Deletion Upon Contract Termination:
Upon the conclusion or termination of this Agreement, the Vendor shall securely remove,
delete, and purge all data related to the Project from the cloud environment and the
vendor has to submit confirmation to the BSTL.

1. Payment Terms
The bidder must accept the payment terms proposed by BSTL. The commercial bid
submitted by the bidders must be in conformity with the payment terms proposed by BSTL.
Any deviation from the proposed payment terms would not be accepted. BSTL shall have
the right to withhold any payment due to the bidder, in case of delays or defaults on the
part of the bidder. Such withholding of payment shall not amount to a default on the part
of BSTL. If any of the items / activities as mentioned in the price bid is not taken up by
BSTL during the assignment, BSTL will not pay the cost of such items and professional
fees quoted by the bidder in the price bid against such activity / item.
The release of payment will be as follows:
The invoices for claiming the payment should be submitted along with the following
documents:
I. Original delivery Challans and related documents dully stamped and signed by the
BSTL Project Manager and vendor representative.
II. Performance Bank Guarantee (PBG) of 5% of Contact value (As per RFP format).
a) Annual Subscription and Maintenance Cost:
100% of yearly subscription and maintenance charges plus applicable tax payable
quarterly in arrears against receipt of satisfactory service report of previous quarter from
the Bank’s Project / Operations Manager.
b) One Time Implementation, Customization & Integration Cost:
i. 10% Payment on submission of Performance Bank Guarantee (PBG) in
accordance with RFP terms. This payment shall be released within 15 days from
the date of acceptance of PBG by BSTL Officials.
ii. 50% of Implementation, Customization & Integration Cost plus applicable tax after
UAT Sign off in the form of Acceptance Test which should be signed by both
BSTL’s identified Project Manager & vendor representative.
iii. 30% of Implementation, Customization & Integration Cost plus applicable tax after
go-live sign off from BSTL. Go Live Sign Off in the form of Acceptance Test should
be signed by both BSTL’s identified Project Manager & vendor representative.
iv. 10% of Implementation, Customization & Integration Cost plus applicable tax after
Go-Live closure signoff from BSTL. Go Live Closure Sign Off in the form of Final
Acceptance Test should be signed by both BSTL’s identified Project Manager &
vendor representative.
c) Cost of additional user licenses subscription block (Optional item):
100% of yearly subscription and maintenance charges of user license’s blocks plus

previous quarter from the Bank’s Project / Operations Manager.
d) Man days for additional customization (Optional item):
Changes suggested by BSTL after implementation and one time customization of the

Price mentioned in the Price Bid shall be the firm price and shall be inclusive of all taxes,
duties, charges and levies etc. and all other payments/expenditures incurred or incurable
in the performance of its obligations under the Purchase Order.
Penalty, if any, shall be deducted as per the penalty clause from the amount payable.
There shall be no escalation in the prices once the prices are fixed and agreed to by
BSTL and the bidder. But any benefit arising out of any subsequent reduction in the
prices due to reduction in duty & taxes after the prices are fixed and before the delivery
should be passed on to BSTL, payments will be released as per above payment terms
on submission of relevant documents. BSTL will pay invoices within a period of 30 days
from the date of receipt of accepted invoices. Any dispute regarding the invoice will be
communicated to vendor within 15 days from the date of receipt of the invoice. After the
dispute is resolved, BSTL shall make payment within 15 days from the date the dispute
stands resolved.

Annexure 03 – Bid Evaluation Terms
1. Evaluation process
There would be a two-stage evaluation process for the selection of vendor.
 Stage 1 – Eligibility Cum Technical Bid Evaluation
 Stage 2 – Commercial Bid Evaluation
During evaluation of the Tenders, BSTL, at its discretion, may ask the Bidder for
clarification in respect of its tender. The request for clarification and the response shall be
in writing, and no change in the substance of the tender shall be sought, offered, or
permitted. BSTL reserves the right to accept or reject any tender in whole or in parts without
assigning any reason thereof. The decision of BSTL shall be final and binding on all the
Bidders to this document and BSTL will not entertain any correspondence in this regard.
A. Eligibility cum Technical Bid Evaluation
Eligibility criterion for the bidder to qualify this stage is clearly mentioned below. The Bidder
would need to provide valid supporting documents as part of the eligibility proof. All dates,
if not specified, to be applicable from the date of the RFP.

its successors and assigns), intends to issue this RFP document, hereinafter called
“RFP”, to eligible Bidders, hereafter called as ‘Bidders’, to participate in the
competitive bidding for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation &
Maintenance of Human Resource Management system for Baroda Sun Technologies
Limited.
2. Project overview
This RFP document has been prepared solely for the purpose of enabling BSTL for
Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of Human
Resource Management system.
BSTL, for this purpose, invites proposal from Vendors who are interested in
participating in this RFP and who fulfil the eligibility criteria mentioned under Annexure
03 and are also in a position to comply with the requirement as mentioned in Project
Scope under Annexure 01.
Apart from the above, vendor must also agree to all the terms & conditions mentioned
under this RFP. The RFP document is not recommendation, offer or invitation to enter
into a contract, agreement or any other arrangement in respect of the services. The
provision of the services is subject to observance of selection process and appropriate
documentation being agreed between BSTL and the successful Vendor to be identified
by BSTL after completion of the selection process as detailed in this document.
BSTL having its Corporate Office at 3rd Floor, Bank of Baroda Building no. 2,
Horniman Circle, Mumbai, India – 400023, provides system integrator / consultancy
services on matters relating to IT enabled business solutions / IT software product
implementation across various lines of business for Bank of Baroda (hereinafter
referred to as “Bank”) and for this it needs to select a Service Provider for Supply,
Implementation & Maintenance of Human Resource Management system and
detailed project scope is enclosed as Annexure 01.

cum technical bid, failing of which, the bid of the concerned bidder shall be rejected.
Bid Security (Earnest Money Deposit)” shall be paid through electronic mode or a Bank
Guarantee (Annexure 05 – Bid Security Form) of an equal amount issued by a
Commercial Bank (other than Bank of Baroda) located in India. This Bid-security is to

Security Money provided by the vendor. The EMD (Earnest Money Deposit) of
successful bidder(s) will be returned on submission of Performance Bank Guarantee
/ security deposit.

reason whatsoever.

a. Exemption for EMD amount:
Exemption from submission of EMD shall be given to bidders, who are Micro, Small
Enterprises (MSE) / Startups. The bidders who are MSE have to submit necessary
document issued by Ministry of MSME Govt of India and the bidders who are startups
have to be recognized by Department for Promotion of Industry and Internal Trade
(DPIIT) to avail the exemption. To qualify for EMD exemption, firms should necessarily
enclose a valid copy of registration certificate issued by Ministry of MSME Govt of India
/ DPIIT which are valid on last date of submission of the tender documents along with
"Bid Security Declaration" accepting that if they withdraw or modify their bids during
period of validity etc., they will be suspended for the time specified in the tender
document. MSE/Startup firms which are in the process of obtaining MSME / DPIIT
certificate will not be considered for EMD exemption.
Since RFP is being floated on GeM Portal, Bid Security related exemptions as
specified in clause 4, Section xiii, Sub-section 'm' of GeM GTC are applicable as the
case may be.
7. Preference to make in India initiative
Government has issued Public Procurement (Preference to Make in India) [PPP-MII]
Order 2017 vide the Department for Promotion of Industry and Internal Trade (DPIIT)
Order No.P-45021/2/2017-B.E.-II dated 15.06.2017 (as revised and amended from
time to time) to encourage ‘Make in India’ and to promote manufacturing and
production of goods, services and works in India with a view to enhancing income and
employment.
Preference will be given with the criteria laid down by Bank and as per procedures laid
down in abovesaid [PPP-MII] order 2017(as revised and amended from time to time)
The guidelines under PPP-MII 2017 order and subsequent revisions/amendment as
mentioned above shall be applicable subject to bidder submitting with Class-I/Class-II
local content certificate for the quoted product.
In case of two or more than two bidders complying with Preference to Make in India
initiative are found eligible as per scope mentioned in Annexure-1 of the RFP, then
Commercial bids of only these bidders will be opened.
In case sufficient bidders complying with Preference to Make in India initiative are not
found eligible as per scope of work mentioned in Annexure-1 of the RFP, then
Commercial bids of all technically eligible bidders will be opened.

9. Delivery Timeline
The Bidder should supply and implement the solution within -6- weeks from the date
of acceptance of the Purchase order. The solution as per the required scope needs to
be rolled out within delivery period mentioned above.
Any deliverable has not been supplied/implemented or not operational on account of
which the implementation is delayed, will be deemed/treated as non-delivery thereby
excluding BSTL from all payment obligations under the terms of this contract.
Bidder will have to pay late delivery charges to BSTL @ 0.1% of the Total Contract
Value inclusive of all taxes, duties, levies etc., per week or part thereof, for late
implementation of the Solution beyond due date of delivery, to a maximum of 5% of
the overall Contract value inclusive of all taxes, duties, levies etc. beyond that BSTL
reserves the right to cancel the contract.

14. Termination
BSTL reserves the right to terminate this RFP at any stage without any notice or
assigning any reason in the event of exceptional circumstances.
At any time during the course of the RFP process or before the award of contract or
after execution of the contract if one or more terms and conditions laid down in this
Request For Proposal has not been met by the bidder or the bidder has made material
misrepresentation or has given any materially incorrect or false information, BSTL may
terminate his contract and may invoke performance bank guarantee or forfeit the
security deposit as the case may be. Further, BSTL may impose such restriction/s on
the defaulting bidder as it may deem fit.
After the award of the contract, if the selected bidder does not perform satisfactorily or
delays execution of the contract, BSTL reserves the right to get the balance contract
executed by another party of its choice by giving one month notice for the same. In
such an event, the bidder is bound to make good the additional expenditure which
BSTL may have to incur for the execution of the balance of the contract.
Notwithstanding above, BSTL reserves the right to terminate this assignment or any
subsequent agreement and / or any particular order, in whole or in part, without
assigning any reason, by giving Service Provider at least 90 days prior notice in writing.

16. Dispute Resolution
BSTL and the Bidder shall make every effort to resolve amicably, by direct informal
negotiation between their respective project managers of, any disagreement or dispute
arising between them under or in connection with this RFP.
If BSTL project manager and Bidder’s project manager/ director are unable to resolve
the dispute within 15 days from the commencement of such informal negotiations, they
shall immediately escalate the dispute to the senior authorized personnel designated
by the Bidder and BSTL respectively.
If within 15 days from the commencement of such negotiations between the senior
authorized personnel, the parties are unable to resolve the dispute amicably, both
parties may refer the dispute to Mediation by mutual consent before the proceedings
of arbitration are resorted to as provided hereinafter. It is made clear that the said
mediation shall be purely optional for both the parties.
Mediation
If both parties mutually agree to resort to/adopt the mediation process the request in
writing would be made by both the parties to any recognised mediation centre at
Mumbai.
Arbitration
In the event the dispute between the parties not getting resolved through the process
mentioned hereinabove, all questions, disputes or differences arising under and out
of, or in connection with the contract or carrying out of the work whether during the
progress of the work or after the completion and whether before or after the
determination, abandonment or breach of the contract shall be referred to arbitration
by a sole Arbitrator acceptable to both parties OR the number of arbitrators shall be
three, with each side to the dispute being entitled to appoint one arbitrator. The two
arbitrators appointed by the parties shall appoint a third arbitrator who shall act as the
chairman of the proceedings.
The seat and place of arbitration shall be Mumbai. The provisions of Arbitration and
Conciliation Act 1996 or any statutory modification thereof shall apply to the arbitration
proceedings.
The arbitral award shall be final and binding on the parties. . Judgment upon the award
may be entered by any court having jurisdiction thereof or having jurisdiction over the
relevant Party.

procurement process or the execution of contract to the detriment of BSTL and
includes collusive practice among Bidders (prior to or after bid submission)
designed to establish bid prices at artificial non-competitive levels and to deprive

Page 24 of 92

Annexure 01 – Project Details (Scope of Work)
1. Broad Scope of work:
BarodaSun Technologies Limited (“BSTL” or “the Company”), a public sector
technology services company, proposes to select a Service Provider for Supply,
Implementation & Maintenance of Human Resource Management system.
BSTL for this purpose, invites proposal from prospective bidders. Bidder who is
interested in participating in this RFP must fulfil the eligibility criteria mentioned in the
RFP and also in a position to comply with the Scope of Work in the document to
provide the required solution. Apart from above the bidder must also agree to all our
terms & conditions mentioned under this RFP.
The proposed Human Resource Management System (HRMS) solution should be
aligned with, but not limited to, the following key features and technical and functional
capabilities:
1. Employee Database Management
1.1 Digital Employee Records & Document Repository: The system should store
complete digital records of every employee, including appointment letters, ID
proofs, certificates, and HR documents.
1.2 Core HRMS (Organization, People, Job data, Position, Job, Contact,
Dependents) & Employee Enrolment: The system should manage all Job Data
actions, including person profiles, personal information, biodata, positions etc. It
should have functionality to enroll and maintain complete official records such as
name, employee ID, date of joining, photo, and update history. The system
should have functionality to create category i.e. Department, Designation,
Division, Projects, Grade, Locality etc and also generate employee, EC No.,
biodata and generic employee lists.
1.3 Personal Details Management: The system should capture address, contact
details, emergency contacts, family information, qualifications, skills, and bank
details with change history.
1.4 Alerts and Notifications: Systematic alerts and notifications in form of emails to
employees and HR/administrative department to update documents, profile
completion, pending updates, etc.
1.5 Multiple Employee Categories: The system should support input and storage of
data for different categories of employees viz Regular, Contractual, and Deputed
staff with relevant information fields.
1.6 Organization Development: The system should enable creation and
management of departments, locations, positions, and job codes. It should

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 25 of 92

maintain the organizational structure, hierarchy mapping , workflow mapping and
reporting relationships.

2. Attendance & Leave Management
2.1 Biometric/Digital Attendance Integration: The system should integrate with
biometric or digital attendance systems to mark attendance of the employee,
which will have functionality to capture thumb impression, IRIS etc. as approved
by Government of India, Government of Maharashtra.
2.2 Office Hours & Holiday Calendar: Maintain office timings, weekly offs, and annual
holiday list.
2.3 Attendance & Leave Reports: There should be functionality to generate daily,
monthly, and yearly attendance summaries and leave details.
2.4 Leave Rules Configuration & Approvals: Creates master BSTL leave rules,
allows online leave application. Approval Level Creation to manage leave
process. Paperless functionality for leave Application by the employees and
approval by their managers with effect to automatic leave ledger update.
2.5 Real-Time Attendance Dashboard & Timesheet: Shows live attendance status,
late marks, early exits. There should be provision for timesheet entries for
employees as per projects. Allow attendance punching with In /Out daily,
monthly, yearly reports.
2.6 Attendance Management: Defines rules for work-from-home, half-day, late
marking, deductions etc as per BSTL’s attendance / leave rules.
3. Payroll, Compensation and Reimbursement & Claims
3.1 CTC Structure & Salary Master: The system should manage employee salary
components and structure.
3.2 Payroll Processing & Statutory Deductions: The system should have provision
for automated payroll processing, tax deductions (TDS, PF, ESI etc). The system
should be integrated with accounting/finance systems, leave details and
attendance master for automatic salary calculations and arrears calculations.
3.3 Investment Declarations & Tax Computation: Allows uploading tax-proof
documents and calculating monthly TDS.
3.4 Salary Slips & Disbursement: Generates payslips and maintains historical salary
records.

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 26 of 92

3.5 Arrears & Adjustment Reports: Supports arrear calculations and related
reporting.
3.6 Claims & Reimbursements – The HRMS should allow employees to submit
claims including TA / DA claims and reimbursement requests as per their
eligibility and policy guidelines. The system should support upload of required
documents, route requests through an approval workflow, and automatically
integrate approved claims with the payroll or payment system for timely
settlement. It should have provision to mark members on leave and reroute
claims selectively. It should also maintain a complete record of all claims with an
audit trail.

4. Employee Self Service & Manager Self Service
4.1 Web and mobile application for employees and managers.
4.2 Employee Self-Service: Employees can apply for leave, view payslips, update
data, etc.
4.3 Manager Self-Service: Managers can approve leave, monitor attendance, and
access reports.
4.4 Notifications: Sends alerts for approvals and pending tasks.
5. Performance Management
5.1 Goal Setting & KPI Tracking: The system should enable creation and tracking
of KPIs and goals.
5.2 Appraisal Cycles & Feedback: The system should support Appraisals cycles,
feedback mechanism and process performance-linked pay.
6. Recruitment & Onboarding
6.1 Job Requisition & Posting: Allows managers to raise hiring requests and HR to
post jobs.
6.2 Applicant Tracking System (ATS) & Candidate Tracking: Manages online
applications, candidate tracking system, shortlisting, and selection stages.
6.3 Interview Scheduling & Selection: Supports scheduling, feedback, and offer
letter generation.
6.4 Onboarding Workflow: Handles document uploads, verification, and employee
code generation.
7. Separation & Exit Management

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 27 of 92

7.1 Resignation Submission: Employees can submit resignations online or on other
separation reasons viz. Demise, Dismissal, etc, the system should follow the
exit process fully automated through workflow including requisite clearances.
7.2 Exit Forms & Clearances: System manages digital exit forms and no-dues
processes.
7.3 Notice Period & Shortfall Calculation: The system should automatically
calculate the notice period, shortfall days with systematic notification / alerts.
7.4 Full & Final Settlement: The system should automatically compute all dues and
recoveries and calculation of full and final settlement.
7.5 Exit Interviews: Records exit feedback.
8. Analytics & Reports
8.1 HR Dashboards: Displays headcount, attendance, attrition, leave usage, etc.
8.2 The system should have ability to generate reports / MIS from system as and
when required. The system should provide flexibility in choosing different
headers for generating various reports.
9. Intimation / Permission:
9.1 Employee Intimation and Permission module. The system should have
provision for employee to request for different types of Intimation / Permission.
10. Confirmation Process:
10.1 The system should generate the list of employees to be confirmed, automatic
workflow with escalation features. On confirmation the record should be
updated automatically in job data.
11. Notice Board / Circulars / Intimation
11.1 The system should have provision for Notice board / Circulars / Intimation tool
– where Admin can post notices/ circulars / letters / communication etc to the
target audience.
12. Full and Final Settlement
12.1 Full and Final Settlement of employees separating from BSTL and calculation
of tax. The FnF should also display any pending dues of the employee.
Payment of F&F records and reconciliation.

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 28 of 92

13. Technical Requirements
a. Web-based and mobile-enabled architecture
b. Cloud-based deployment.
c. High availability, scalability, and disaster recovery support.
d. Role-based access control and data security as per prevailing standards.
e. Compatibility with modern browsers and operating systems.
14. Training (Offline / Online)
a. User training for HR, IT, and administrative staff
b. Preparation of user manuals and training materials
c. Post-implementation support and capacity building
15. Support and Maintenance
a. Comprehensive warranty and AMC support post go-live
b. Helpdesk support (email, ticketing, remote assistance)
c. Periodic upgrades, patches, and system performance reviews

3. Implementation & Customization
Bidder may require doing some customization in existing module of their software to
meet the requirement of BSLT which should be cover as part of implementation. The
system should provide Confidentiality, Integrity, Authentication and Non-Repudiation
as per current standard (Signing, Hashing, Time-stamping and Encryption).
Bidder as part of the implementation phase shall have to provide a comprehensive
hands-on online / Offline training to BSTL officials at the location prescribed by the
BSTL. The objective of training would be to make an individual familiarise with solution
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 33 of 92

& to get start working on it. The training shall cover the complete process. The
successful completion of the training would be deemed as executed as part of
acceptance of Project Sign off.
After successful completion of the implementation phase, Bidder is liable to provide Offline /
onsite training to BSTL when required during the contract period. There will not be any cost
borne by BSTL for any training.
All out of pocket expenses, traveling, boarding and lodging expenses for the entire life of the
contract should be a part of the commercial bid submitted by the vendor to the BSTL. No extra
costs on account of any items or services or by way of any out of pocket expenses, including
travel, boarding and lodging etc. will be payable by BSTL. The vendor cannot take the plea of
omitting any charges or costs and later lodge a claim on the BSTL for the same.

The vendor have to arrange for customization if required in the system to meet the
changes suggested by CVC/ RBI/ Govt. of India/ regulatory authorities/BSTL towards
compliance without any additional cost during the contract period
However, if any changes suggested by BSTL after implementation and one time
customization of the system, BSTL will freeze the manpower effort, after mutual
discussion with the successful vendor.
Vendor need to freeze and sign SOW (scope of work) for customization required by
BSTL on mutual discussion as a payment milestone.
4. Data Segregation and Security Audits:
BSTL's data should be completely segregated within the Cloud environment. The
vendor has to submit CERT-IN empanelled auditor confirmation on BSTL’s data
segregation controls complying to the regulatory norms.
5. Vulnerability Assessment and Penetration Testing (VAPT):
The Vendor shall conduct quarterly vulnerability assessments (VA) on the Cloud
application and its underlying infrastructure.
In addition to the above vulnerability assessments, the Vendor shall conduct Yearly
comprehensive Penetration Testing (PT) on the Cloud application and its underlying
infrastructure.
The Vendor shall provide to the BSTL with a detailed compliance report outlining the
findings of the vulnerability assessments and Penetration Testing, including identified
vulnerabilities, the associated risk level, and the remediation.
However, Bidder must support if BSTL wants to conduct the above assessment
through its technical vendor as and when need arises.
6. Data Migration:
In the event of any future migration of data, the Service Provider will provide complete
access and support to the BSTL. BSTL will decide migration approach. There will not
be any commercial attach with the activity.
7. Data Deletion Upon Contract Termination:

Downloading of Tender Document

The tender document is uploaded / released on GeM Portal. Tender document and

The Tender document will be available online only. Tender document will not be sold

Preparation & Submission of Bids

The bids (Pre-Qualification, Eligibility, Technical as well as Commercial) shall have to
be prepared and subsequently submitted online only. Bids not submitted “ONLINE” or
by any other mean shall be summarily rejected. No other form of submission shall be
permitted.

Do’s and Don’ts for Bidder

 Registration process for new Bidders should be completed within first week of
release of tender.

BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 37 of 92

 The GeM Portal is open for upload of documents from the start of the bid
submission date. Hence bidders are advised to start the process of upload of bid
documents well in advance.
 Bidders have to prepare for submission of their bid documents online well in
advance as
▪ The encrypt/upload process of soft copy of the bid documents large in number to
the portal may take longer time depending upon bidder’s infrastructure and
connectivity.
▪ To avoid last minute rush & technical difficulties faced by bidders in
uploading/submission of bids, bidders are required to start the uploading of all
the required documents -01- week in advance for timely online submission of bid.
 Bidders to initiate uploading of few primary documents during the start of the tender
submission and any request for help/support required for uploading the documents
/ understanding the system should be taken up with GeM authorities well in
advance.
 Bidders should not raise request for extension of time on the last day of submission
due to non-submission of their bids on time as BSTL will not be in a position to
provide any support at the last minute as the portal is managed by GeM authorities.
 Bidder should not raise request for offline submission or late submission since
ONLINE submission is accepted only.
 Partly or incomplete submission of bids by the bidders will not be processed and
will be summarily rejected.

Annexure 03 – Bid Evaluation Terms
1. Evaluation process
There would be a two-stage evaluation process for the selection of vendor.
 Stage 1 – Eligibility Cum Technical Bid Evaluation
 Stage 2 – Commercial Bid Evaluation
During evaluation of the Tenders, BSTL, at its discretion, may ask the Bidder for
clarification in respect of its tender. The request for clarification and the response shall
be in writing, and no change in the substance of the tender shall be sought, offered,
or permitted. BSTL reserves the right to accept or reject any tender in whole or in parts
without assigning any reason thereof. The decision of BSTL shall be final and binding
on all the Bidders to this document and BSTL will not entertain any correspondence in
this regard.

C. Commercial Bid Evaluation
The commercial bids of only those bidders who qualify in both eligibility and technical
evaluation will be opened. The provision of Make in India preferences would be
considered while opening and finalizing of bid as provided in this RFP. The
commercial bids submitted by Bidders will be evaluated based on discounted total
cost of ownership. The discount rate will be used @ 10%. The key considerations of
the TCO would be the total payouts for entire project through the contract period,
discounted at 10% to arrive at the present value of the future cash outflows.
Accordingly, the L1 (Lowest Bidder) would be arrived at. The calculation performed
by BSTL in this regard is as follows:
•

The discounted rate will be calculated on yearly basis based on the formula
A/(1+i/100)^n where A= Total Value in each Year; i=10% and n =Year.

•

The Present Value will be calculated for all components where the payment is
recurring year on year. The Present Value for the component will start from the
year of purchase of that component / start of the services (AMC) and shall be
calculated till the end year of the contract. Further n - number of period will be ‘0’
in the year of purchase of that component / start of the services and subsequently
increased by 1 for subsequent years.

Any component / service for which the payment is a One Time Cost the NPV cost of
the equipment / service for that year will be considered and the relevant year’s NPV
cost will be added as part of Present Value calculation for that year. Further the
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 44 of 92

payment of the OTC component / services is not recurring in nature hence the present
value for that component / services will be considered in the year of purchase only
and not in subsequent years.
The decision of BSTL shall be final and binding on all the bidders to this document.
BSTL reserves the right to accept or reject an offer without assigning any reason
whatsoever. The bidder is expected not to add any conditions / deviations in the
commercial bid. Any such conditions / deviations may make the bid liable for
disqualification.
The proposal of L1 (Lowest quote) bidder shall be recommended for award of
contract.
2. Commercial Bids Terms
In the event BSTL has not asked for any quotes for alternative prices, and the bidder
furnishes the alternative price in the Bidder’s financial bid, the higher of the prices will
be taken for calculating and arriving at the Total Cost of Ownership. However, payment
by BSTL will be made at the lower price. BSTL in this case may also reject the offer
outright.
In the event optional prices are not quoted by the bidder, for items where such prices
are a must and required to be quoted for, the highest price quoted by any of the
participating Bidder will be taken as the costs, for such alternatives and also for arriving
at the Total Cost of Ownership for the purpose of evaluation of the Bidder. The same
item has to be supplied by the Bidder free of cost.
The bidder is requested to quote in Indian Rupee (INR). Bids in currencies other than
INR would not be considered. The date for opening of price bids would be
communicated separately to the successful bidders post the completion of the
technical evaluation (If bid opening takes place through BSTL’ eProcurement portal).
The prices and other terms offered by bidders must be firm for an acceptance period
of 180 days from the opening of the commercial bid.
In case of any variation (upward or down ward) in Government levies / taxes / cess /
duties etc. which has been included as part of the price will be borne by the bidder.
Variation would also include the introduction of any new tax / cess/ duty, etc., provided
that the benefit or burden of other taxes quoted separately as part of the commercial
bid like GST and any taxes introduced instead of GST and levies associated to GST
or any new taxes introduced after the submission of bidder’s proposal shall be passed
on or adjusted to BSTL. If the Bidder makes any conditional or vague offers, without
conforming to these guidelines, BSTL will treat the prices quoted as in conformity with
these guidelines and proceed accordingly. Necessary documentary evidence should
be produced for having paid any tax/cess/duty, if applicable, and/or other applicable
levies.

commercial bids once again for scrutiny. BSTL can repeat this normalization process
at every stage of bid submission or till BSTL is satisfied. The bidders agree that they
have no reservation or objection to the normalization process and all the bidders will,
by responding to this RFP, agree to participate in the normalization process and
extend their co-operation to BSTL during this process. The bidders, by submitting the
response to this RFP, agree to the process and conditions of the normalization
process.

Annexure 13 – Binding Criteria for Technical Presentation
Re: RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance
of Human Resource Management system.

technical presentations during the bid selection process:
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•

At least one senior representative from bidder’s organization should be present
in the technical presentations
Complete soft copies of the presentation materials should be shared with BSTL
before the presentation.
BSTL will expect and demand that the key personnel showcased by the vendor
in the technical presentation should be the same ones that actively drive the
project execution.
Bidders must strictly adhere to the time slots provided (Approx. 75 Minutes) to
them for the technical presentation, allowing ample time and scope for
question-answers.
Focus of the presentation should be on the specifics of the approach /
methodology being proposed for BSTL.
Both technical and administrative aspects of the project should be given
suitable coverage.
All aspects of requirements in the RFP should be covered in the presentation.
All RFP requirements that are not covered by the proposed plan, or not adhered
to, should be clearly brought out in the technical presentation, along with
reasons / justification on why they will not be covered.
Any assumption, if taken in the response to RFP document should be clearly
brought out in the technical presentation, along with the justification.
BSTL’s evaluation and scoring on all aspects including technical presentation
are final and non-negotiable.

Annexure 14 – Service levels.
The Vendor understands the scale of this Project and that it would require tremendous
commitment of financial and technical resources for the same, for the tenure of
Contract under this RFP.
BSTL expects that the successful Vendor to adhere to the following minimum Service
Levels:
▪ Any fault/ issue/ defect failure intimated by BSTL through any mode of
communication like call/e-mail/sms etc. are to be acted upon, so as to adhere to
the service levels. Business/ Service Downtime and Deterioration shall be the key
considerations for determining “Penalties” that would be levied on the Successful
Vendor.
▪ The Vendor should have 24X7 Management, escalation and resolution
infrastructure.
▪ Time bound problem addressing team (onsite/offsite) for the complete contract
period.
▪ Vendor to arrange for updation required in the system to meet the changes
suggested by RBI/ Govt. of India/ regulatory authorities towards compliance as
part of support at no extra cost to BSTL for the entire contract period. Any delay
in meeting the timelines would result in penalty.
Vendor will have to guarantee a minimum uptime of 99.9%, calculated on a monthly
basis. Application (As a whole / any module of the application) availability will be 99.9%
on 24x7x365. The penalty will be calculated as per the details given below:
Uptime percentage - 100% less Downtime Percentage
Downtime percentage - Unavailable Time divided by Total Available Time, calculated
on a monthly basis.
Total Available Time – 24 hrs per day for seven days a week excluding planned
downtime.
Unavailable Time - Time involved while the solution is inoperative or operates
inconsistently or erratically.
Uptime Percentage
Penalty Details
A >= 99.9%
No Penalty
99.9% > A >=99.5%
2% of cost of monthly Subscription charges
99.5% > A >= 99.0%
5% of cost of monthly Subscription charges
Penalty at an incremental rate of 1% (in addition to a
99.0% > A
base of 5%) of cost of monthly Subscription charges for
every 0.1% lower than the stipulated uptime.
The uptime percentage would be calculated on monthly basis and the calculated
amount would be adjusted from every subsequent quarterly payment. The SLA
charges will be subject to an overall cap of 10% of the Yearly subscription Charges
and thereafter, Bank has the discretion to cancel the contract. If Vendor materially fails
to meet an uptime of 99.9% for three (3) consecutive months, the Bank may have the
right to terminate the contract. In case if there is no pending invoices to be paid by the
Bank to the vendor, the vendor has to submit a pay order / cheque payable at Mumbai

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 70 of 92

in favour of Bank of Baroda for the same within 15 days from the notice period from
the Bank. Solution downtime occurring due to factors attributable to the Bank and
acknowledged by Bank will not impact the SLA.

Order No.P-45021/2/2017-B.E.-II dated 15.06.2017 (as revised and amended from

production of goods, services and works in India with a view to enhancing income and
employment.
Preference will be given with the criteria laid down by Bank and as per procedures laid
down in abovesaid [PPP-MII] order 2017(as revised and amended from time to time)
The guidelines under PPP-MII 2017 order and subsequent revisions/amendment as
mentioned above shall be applicable subject to bidder submitting with Class-I/Class-II
local content certificate for the quoted product.
In case of two or more than two bidders complying with Preference to Make in India
initiative are found eligible as per scope mentioned in Annexure-1 of the RFP, then
Commercial bids of only these bidders will be opened.
In case sufficient bidders complying with Preference to Make in India initiative are not
found eligible as per scope of work mentioned in Annexure-1 of the RFP, then
Commercial bids of all technically eligible bidders will be opened.

Note: Payment will be released on submission of unconditional signoff from BSTL
Official along with original invoice after submission of PBG and signing of SLA
(including an NDA by Successful Bidder).
Price mentioned in the Price Bid shall be the firm price and shall be inclusive of all
taxes, duties, charges and levies etc. and all other payments/expenditures incurred or
incurable in the performance of its obligations under the Purchase Order.
Penalty, if any, shall be deducted as per the penalty clause from the amount payable.
There shall be no escalation in the prices once the prices are fixed and agreed to by
BSTL and the bidder. But any benefit arising out of any subsequent reduction in the
prices due to reduction in duty & taxes after the prices are fixed and before the delivery
should be passed on to BSTL, payments will be released as per above payment terms
on submission of relevant documents. BSTL will pay invoices within a period of 30
days from the date of receipt of accepted invoices. Any dispute regarding the invoice
will be communicated to vendor within 15 days from the date of receipt of the invoice.
After the dispute is resolved, BSTL shall make payment within 15 days from the date
the dispute stands resolved.

16. Dispute Resolution
BSTL and the Bidder shall make every effort to resolve amicably, by direct informal
negotiation between their respective project managers of, any disagreement or dispute
arising between them under or in connection with this RFP.
If BSTL project manager and Bidder’s project manager/ director are unable to resolve
the dispute within 15 days from the commencement of such informal negotiations, they
shall immediately escalate the dispute to the senior authorized personnel designated
by the Bidder and BSTL respectively.
If within 15 days from the commencement of such negotiations between the senior
authorized personnel, the parties are unable to resolve the dispute amicably, both
parties may refer the dispute to Mediation by mutual consent before the proceedings
of arbitration are resorted to as provided hereinafter. It is made clear that the said
mediation shall be purely optional for both the parties.
Mediation
If both parties mutually agree to resort to/adopt the mediation process the request in
writing would be made by both the parties to any recognised mediation centre at
Mumbai.
Arbitration
In the event the dispute between the parties not getting resolved through the process
mentioned hereinabove, all questions, disputes or differences arising under and out
of, or in connection with the contract or carrying out of the work whether during the
progress of the work or after the completion and whether before or after the
determination, abandonment or breach of the contract shall be referred to arbitration
by a sole Arbitrator acceptable to both parties OR the number of arbitrators shall be
three, with each side to the dispute being entitled to appoint one arbitrator. The two
arbitrators appointed by the parties shall appoint a third arbitrator who shall act as the
chairman of the proceedings.
The seat and place of arbitration shall be Mumbai. The provisions of Arbitration and
Conciliation Act 1996 or any statutory modification thereof shall apply to the arbitration
proceedings.
The arbitral award shall be final and binding on the parties. . Judgment upon the award
may be entered by any court having jurisdiction thereof or having jurisdiction over the
relevant Party.

18. Prevention of Corrupt and Fraudulent Practices
Every Bidders / Suppliers / Contractors is expected to observe the highest standard of
ethics during the procurement and execution of such contracts in pursuance of the
policy:
• “Corrupt Practice” means the offering, giving, receiving or soliciting of anything of
value to influence the action of an official in the procurement / bidding process or in
contract execution AND
• “Fraudulent Practice” means a misrepresentation of facts in order to influence a
procurement process or the execution of contract to the detriment of BSTL and
includes collusive practice among Bidders (prior to or after bid submission)
designed to establish bid prices at artificial non-competitive levels and to deprive
BSTL of the benefits of free and open competition.
BSTL reserves the right to reject a proposal for award of contract to any successful
bidder if it has prima facie found sufficient evidence/proof of such bidder having
engaged in corrupt or fraudulent practices in competing for the contract in question.
BSTL further reserves the right to declare a firm ineligible, either indefinitely or for a
stated period of time, to be awarded a contract if at any time it has prima facie found
sufficient evidence/proof of such firm having engaged in corrupt or fraudulent practices
in competing for or in executing the contract.
Successful vendor/Service Provider, to be selected according to the
service/work/project for which this Request for proposal is invited shall comply with
BSTL’s / BOB’s Code of Ethics which is available on BSTL’s / BOB’s
website>Shareholder’s Corner> Policies/Codes> Our Code of Ethics.

BSTL, or for any kind of income or benefits. The Bidder alone shall file all
applicable tax returns for all of its personnel assigned hereunder in a manner
consistent with its status as an independent contractor of services; and the

manner.

Annexure 01 – Project Details (Scope of Work)
1. Broad Scope of work:
BarodaSun Technologies Limited (“BSTL” or “the Company”), a public sector
technology services company, proposes to select a Service Provider for Supply,
Implementation & Maintenance of Human Resource Management system.
BSTL for this purpose, invites proposal from prospective bidders. Bidder who is
interested in participating in this RFP must fulfil the eligibility criteria mentioned in the
RFP and also in a position to comply with the Scope of Work in the document to
provide the required solution. Apart from above the bidder must also agree to all our
terms & conditions mentioned under this RFP.
The proposed Human Resource Management System (HRMS) solution should be
aligned with, but not limited to, the following key features and technical and functional
capabilities:
1. Employee Database Management
1.1 Digital Employee Records & Document Repository: The system should store
complete digital records of every employee, including appointment letters, ID
proofs, certificates, and HR documents.
1.2 Core HRMS (Organization, People, Job data, Position, Job, Contact,
Dependents) & Employee Enrolment: The system should manage all Job Data
actions, including person profiles, personal information, biodata, positions etc. It
should have functionality to enroll and maintain complete official records such as
name, employee ID, date of joining, photo, and update history. The system
should have functionality to create category i.e. Department, Designation,
Division, Projects, Grade, Locality etc and also generate employee, EC No.,
biodata and generic employee lists.
1.3 Personal Details Management: The system should capture address, contact
details, emergency contacts, family information, qualifications, skills, and bank
details with change history.
1.4 Alerts and Notifications: Systematic alerts and notifications in form of emails to
employees and HR/administrative department to update documents, profile
completion, pending updates, etc.
1.5 Multiple Employee Categories: The system should support input and storage of
data for different categories of employees viz Regular, Contractual, and Deputed
staff with relevant information fields.
1.6 Organization Development: The system should enable creation and
management of departments, locations, positions, and job codes. It should

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 25 of 92

maintain the organizational structure, hierarchy mapping , workflow mapping and
reporting relationships.

2. Attendance & Leave Management
2.1 Biometric/Digital Attendance Integration: The system should integrate with
biometric or digital attendance systems to mark attendance of the employee,
which will have functionality to capture thumb impression, IRIS etc. as approved
by Government of India, Government of Maharashtra.
2.2 Office Hours & Holiday Calendar: Maintain office timings, weekly offs, and annual
holiday list.
2.3 Attendance & Leave Reports: There should be functionality to generate daily,
monthly, and yearly attendance summaries and leave details.
2.4 Leave Rules Configuration & Approvals: Creates master BSTL leave rules,
allows online leave application. Approval Level Creation to manage leave
process. Paperless functionality for leave Application by the employees and
approval by their managers with effect to automatic leave ledger update.
2.5 Real-Time Attendance Dashboard & Timesheet: Shows live attendance status,
late marks, early exits. There should be provision for timesheet entries for
employees as per projects. Allow attendance punching with In /Out daily,
monthly, yearly reports.
2.6 Attendance Management: Defines rules for work-from-home, half-day, late
marking, deductions etc as per BSTL’s attendance / leave rules.
3. Payroll, Compensation and Reimbursement & Claims
3.1 CTC Structure & Salary Master: The system should manage employee salary
components and structure.
3.2 Payroll Processing & Statutory Deductions: The system should have provision
for automated payroll processing, tax deductions (TDS, PF, ESI etc). The system
should be integrated with accounting/finance systems, leave details and
attendance master for automatic salary calculations and arrears calculations.
3.3 Investment Declarations & Tax Computation: Allows uploading tax-proof
documents and calculating monthly TDS.
3.4 Salary Slips & Disbursement: Generates payslips and maintains historical salary
records.

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 26 of 92

3.5 Arrears & Adjustment Reports: Supports arrear calculations and related
reporting.
3.6 Claims & Reimbursements – The HRMS should allow employees to submit
claims including TA / DA claims and reimbursement requests as per their
eligibility and policy guidelines. The system should support upload of required
documents, route requests through an approval workflow, and automatically
integrate approved claims with the payroll or payment system for timely
settlement. It should have provision to mark members on leave and reroute
claims selectively. It should also maintain a complete record of all claims with an
audit trail.

4. Employee Self Service & Manager Self Service
4.1 Web and mobile application for employees and managers.
4.2 Employee Self-Service: Employees can apply for leave, view payslips, update
data, etc.
4.3 Manager Self-Service: Managers can approve leave, monitor attendance, and
access reports.
4.4 Notifications: Sends alerts for approvals and pending tasks.
5. Performance Management
5.1 Goal Setting & KPI Tracking: The system should enable creation and tracking
of KPIs and goals.
5.2 Appraisal Cycles & Feedback: The system should support Appraisals cycles,
feedback mechanism and process performance-linked pay.
6. Recruitment & Onboarding
6.1 Job Requisition & Posting: Allows managers to raise hiring requests and HR to
post jobs.
6.2 Applicant Tracking System (ATS) & Candidate Tracking: Manages online
applications, candidate tracking system, shortlisting, and selection stages.
6.3 Interview Scheduling & Selection: Supports scheduling, feedback, and offer
letter generation.
6.4 Onboarding Workflow: Handles document uploads, verification, and employee
code generation.
7. Separation & Exit Management

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 27 of 92

7.1 Resignation Submission: Employees can submit resignations online or on other
separation reasons viz. Demise, Dismissal, etc, the system should follow the
exit process fully automated through workflow including requisite clearances.
7.2 Exit Forms & Clearances: System manages digital exit forms and no-dues
processes.
7.3 Notice Period & Shortfall Calculation: The system should automatically
calculate the notice period, shortfall days with systematic notification / alerts.
7.4 Full & Final Settlement: The system should automatically compute all dues and
recoveries and calculation of full and final settlement.
7.5 Exit Interviews: Records exit feedback.
8. Analytics & Reports
8.1 HR Dashboards: Displays headcount, attendance, attrition, leave usage, etc.
8.2 The system should have ability to generate reports / MIS from system as and
when required. The system should provide flexibility in choosing different
headers for generating various reports.
9. Intimation / Permission:
9.1 Employee Intimation and Permission module. The system should have
provision for employee to request for different types of Intimation / Permission.
10. Confirmation Process:
10.1 The system should generate the list of employees to be confirmed, automatic
workflow with escalation features. On confirmation the record should be
updated automatically in job data.
11. Notice Board / Circulars / Intimation
11.1 The system should have provision for Notice board / Circulars / Intimation tool
– where Admin can post notices/ circulars / letters / communication etc to the
target audience.
12. Full and Final Settlement
12.1 Full and Final Settlement of employees separating from BSTL and calculation
of tax. The FnF should also display any pending dues of the employee.
Payment of F&F records and reconciliation.

RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 28 of 92

13. Technical Requirements
a. Web-based and mobile-enabled architecture
b. Cloud-based deployment.
c. High availability, scalability, and disaster recovery support.
d. Role-based access control and data security as per prevailing standards.
e. Compatibility with modern browsers and operating systems.
14. Training (Offline / Online)
a. User training for HR, IT, and administrative staff
b. Preparation of user manuals and training materials
c. Post-implementation support and capacity building
15. Support and Maintenance
a. Comprehensive warranty and AMC support post go-live
b. Helpdesk support (email, ticketing, remote assistance)
c. Periodic upgrades, patches, and system performance reviews

3. Implementation & Customization
Bidder may require doing some customization in existing module of their software to
meet the requirement of BSLT which should be cover as part of implementation. The
system should provide Confidentiality, Integrity, Authentication and Non-Repudiation
as per current standard (Signing, Hashing, Time-stamping and Encryption).
Bidder as part of the implementation phase shall have to provide a comprehensive
hands-on online / Offline training to BSTL officials at the location prescribed by the
BSTL. The objective of training would be to make an individual familiarise with solution
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 33 of 92

& to get start working on it. The training shall cover the complete process. The
successful completion of the training would be deemed as executed as part of
acceptance of Project Sign off.
After successful completion of the implementation phase, Bidder is liable to provide Offline /
onsite training to BSTL when required during the contract period. There will not be any cost
borne by BSTL for any training.
All out of pocket expenses, traveling, boarding and lodging expenses for the entire life of the
contract should be a part of the commercial bid submitted by the vendor to the BSTL. No extra
costs on account of any items or services or by way of any out of pocket expenses, including
travel, boarding and lodging etc. will be payable by BSTL. The vendor cannot take the plea of
omitting any charges or costs and later lodge a claim on the BSTL for the same.

The vendor have to arrange for customization if required in the system to meet the
changes suggested by CVC/ RBI/ Govt. of India/ regulatory authorities/BSTL towards
compliance without any additional cost during the contract period
However, if any changes suggested by BSTL after implementation and one time
customization of the system, BSTL will freeze the manpower effort, after mutual
discussion with the successful vendor.
Vendor need to freeze and sign SOW (scope of work) for customization required by
BSTL on mutual discussion as a payment milestone.
4. Data Segregation and Security Audits:
BSTL's data should be completely segregated within the Cloud environment. The
vendor has to submit CERT-IN empanelled auditor confirmation on BSTL’s data
segregation controls complying to the regulatory norms.
5. Vulnerability Assessment and Penetration Testing (VAPT):
The Vendor shall conduct quarterly vulnerability assessments (VA) on the Cloud
application and its underlying infrastructure.
In addition to the above vulnerability assessments, the Vendor shall conduct Yearly
comprehensive Penetration Testing (PT) on the Cloud application and its underlying
infrastructure.
The Vendor shall provide to the BSTL with a detailed compliance report outlining the
findings of the vulnerability assessments and Penetration Testing, including identified
vulnerabilities, the associated risk level, and the remediation.
However, Bidder must support if BSTL wants to conduct the above assessment
through its technical vendor as and when need arises.
6. Data Migration:
In the event of any future migration of data, the Service Provider will provide complete
access and support to the BSTL. BSTL will decide migration approach. There will not
be any commercial attach with the activity.
7. Data Deletion Upon Contract Termination:

1. Evaluation process
There would be a two-stage evaluation process for the selection of vendor.
 Stage 1 – Eligibility Cum Technical Bid Evaluation
 Stage 2 – Commercial Bid Evaluation
During evaluation of the Tenders, BSTL, at its discretion, may ask the Bidder for
clarification in respect of its tender. The request for clarification and the response shall
be in writing, and no change in the substance of the tender shall be sought, offered,
or permitted. BSTL reserves the right to accept or reject any tender in whole or in parts
without assigning any reason thereof. The decision of BSTL shall be final and binding
on all the Bidders to this document and BSTL will not entertain any correspondence in
this regard.

In case there is only one vendor having technical score of 70 or more, BSTL may, at

proposed solution does not meet BSTL’s functional and technical requirement.
C. Commercial Bid Evaluation
The commercial bids of only those bidders who qualify in both eligibility and technical
evaluation will be opened. The provision of Make in India preferences would be
considered while opening and finalizing of bid as provided in this RFP. The
commercial bids submitted by Bidders will be evaluated based on discounted total
cost of ownership. The discount rate will be used @ 10%. The key considerations of
the TCO would be the total payouts for entire project through the contract period,
discounted at 10% to arrive at the present value of the future cash outflows.
Accordingly, the L1 (Lowest Bidder) would be arrived at. The calculation performed
by BSTL in this regard is as follows:
•

The discounted rate will be calculated on yearly basis based on the formula
A/(1+i/100)^n where A= Total Value in each Year; i=10% and n =Year.

•

The Present Value will be calculated for all components where the payment is
recurring year on year. The Present Value for the component will start from the
year of purchase of that component / start of the services (AMC) and shall be
calculated till the end year of the contract. Further n - number of period will be ‘0’
in the year of purchase of that component / start of the services and subsequently
increased by 1 for subsequent years.

Any component / service for which the payment is a One Time Cost the NPV cost of
the equipment / service for that year will be considered and the relevant year’s NPV
cost will be added as part of Present Value calculation for that year. Further the
RFP for Selection of Service Provider for Supply, Implementation & Maintenance of
Human Resource Management system.
BarodaSun Technologies Ltd.

Page 44 of 92

payment of the OTC component / services is not recurring in nature hence the present
value for that component / services will be considered in the year of purchase only
and not in subsequent years.
The decision of BSTL shall be final and binding on all the bidders to this document.
BSTL reserves the right to accept or reject an offer without assigning any reason
whatsoever. The bidder is expected not to add any conditions / deviations in the
commercial bid. Any such conditions / deviations may make the bid liable for
disqualification.
The proposal of L1 (Lowest quote) bidder shall be recommended for award of
contract.
2. Commercial Bids Terms
In the event BSTL has not asked for any quotes for alternative prices, and the bidder
furnishes the alternative price in the Bidder’s financial bid, the higher of the prices will
be taken for calculating and arriving at the Total Cost of Ownership. However, payment
by BSTL will be made at the lower price. BSTL in this case may also reject the offer
outright.
In the event optional prices are not quoted by the bidder, for items where such prices
are a must and required to be quoted for, the highest price quoted by any of the
participating Bidder will be taken as the costs, for such alternatives and also for arriving
at the Total Cost of Ownership for the purpose of evaluation of the Bidder. The same
item has to be supplied by the Bidder free of cost.
The bidder is requested to quote in Indian Rupee (INR). Bids in currencies other than
INR would not be considered. The date for opening of price bids would be
communicated separately to the successful bidders post the completion of the
technical evaluation (If bid opening takes place through BSTL’ eProcurement portal).
The prices and other terms offered by bidders must be firm for an acceptance period
of 180 days from the opening of the commercial bid.
In case of any variation (upward or down ward) in Government levies / taxes / cess /
duties etc. which has been included as part of the price will be borne by the bidder.
Variation would also include the introduction of any new tax / cess/ duty, etc., provided
that the benefit or burden of other taxes quoted separately as part of the commercial
bid like GST and any taxes introduced instead of GST and levies associated to GST
or any new taxes introduced after the submission of bidder’s proposal shall be passed
on or adjusted to BSTL. If the Bidder makes any conditional or vague offers, without
conforming to these guidelines, BSTL will treat the prices quoted as in conformity with
these guidelines and proceed accordingly. Necessary documentary evidence should
be produced for having paid any tax/cess/duty, if applicable, and/or other applicable
levies.
If any Tax authorities of any state, including, Local authorities like Corporation,
Municipality etc. or any Government authority or Statutory or autonomous or such
other authority imposes any tax, charge or levy or any cess / charge other than GST
and if BSTL has to pay the same for any of the items or supplies made here under by

any such assumptions.

3. Price Comparisons
The successful bidder will be determined on the basis of bid evaluation process as
mentioned under Evaluation Criteria in this RFP document.
Normalization of bids: BSTL will go through a process of evaluation and normalization
of the bids to the extent possible and feasible to ensure that bidders are more or less
on the same ground of evaluation. After the normalization process, if BSTL feels that
any of the bids needs to be normalized and that such normalization has a bearing on
the price bids; BSTL may at its discretion ask all the shortlisted bidders to resubmit the
commercial bids once again for scrutiny. BSTL can repeat this normalization process
at every stage of bid submission or till BSTL is satisfied. The bidders agree that they
have no reservation or objection to the normalization process and all the bidders will,
by responding to this RFP, agree to participate in the normalization process and
extend their co-operation to BSTL during this process. The bidders, by submitting the
response to this RFP, agree to the process and conditions of the normalization
process.
The Price offer shall be on a fixed price basis. Bid submitted with an adjustable price
quotation will be treated as non-responsive and will be liable to be rejected. The rate
quoted by the bidder should necessarily include the following:
Prices quoted by the Bidder should be inclusive of all taxes, duties, levies, GST etc.
The Bidder shall provide the GST amount in the commercial bids (without amounts
being submitted in the technical response). There will be no price escalation during
the contract period and any extension thereof.
The Bidders are expected to provide details of services which are required to be
extended by the Bidder in accordance with the terms and conditions of the contract.
The Bidder must provide and quote for the required product and services as desired
by BSTL as mentioned in this RFP. Any product or services not proposed to be
provided by the Bidder will result in the proposal being incomplete, which may lead to
disqualification of the Bidder.
